Publisher: A Paris, chez les Libraires associés
Publication Date: 1790
Rights: Public Domain (published 1790, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item lesopusculesspir01guyo (https://archive.org/details/lesopusculesspir01guyo), the item's own lesopusculesspir01guyo_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Fisher - University of Toronto; host metadata date: 1790. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 17e5f5834b956689d71e2a09fe53644c8ed7934fd4b54d260fd62fb1d10c9b1f.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Toronto (Fisher Library) scan; good OCR, long s as f.
Content note: The editor's general preface defending Guyon's writings; the Moyen court et très facile de faire oraison (1685) with its approbation; a letter of Juan Falconi; the Courte apologie pour le Moyen court; and Les Torrens spirituels.
